Specifically, LA4VLA decomposes expert demonstration trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each segment with a corresponding low-level action description. This yields LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K Language-Action (LA) episodes derived entirely from existing demonstrations without additional robot data collection. We further develop LA4VLA-1B, a lightweight 1B-parameter VLA model, and investigate three paradigms for incorporating language-action supervision into VLA learning: LA-only pretraining, sequential LA-to-VLA pretraining, and mixed LA-VLA pretraining. Across simulation and real-world tasks, LA-pretrained policies consistently outperform matched VLA-pretrained counterparts, while combining LA and VLA supervision leads to further gains. In particular, mixed LA-VLA pretraining improves the average success rate of LA4VLA-1B over the no-pretraining baseline by up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage points in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. These results establish LA4VLA as an effective and complementary pretraining strategy for building stronger and more robust VLA policies.

</details>


### [144] [VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27344)
*Yuemin Mao,Uksang Yoo,Jean Oh,Jonathan Francis,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VibeAct提出了一种框架，通过在真实世界嵌入压电麦克风收集振动声学信号，并在仿真中利用共享的接触与滑移物理表示来训练灵巧操作策略，无需模拟原始音频即可实现从仿真到真实的策略迁移，在多种接触密集型任务中优于传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖快速、局部且常被视觉遮挡的接触事件。压电麦克风虽能高带宽感知这些交互，但其振动声学信号难以在仿真中端到端模拟，导致仿真到真实的策略学习存在困难。

Method: 在真实灵巧手上嵌入压电麦克风，通过遥操作收集振动声学数据；在标定的数字孪生中重放记录，自动标注每根手指的接触与滑移标签；训练触觉估计器从真实麦克风波形预测接触与滑移；在仿真中基于相同表示（从仿真接触直接计算）训练操作策略，避免模拟原始音频。

Result: 在重抓取、手中重定向和插入等五种接触密集型任务中，VibeAct在仿真中一致优于基于本体感觉与点云的基线方法，在需要持续反应控制的任务上提升最大（连续滑移幅度通道信息最有效）。学到的策略成功迁移到真实灵巧手-臂平台，提高了部署任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过共享接触与滑移的物理表示，VibeAct有效桥接了真实振动触觉感知与仿真强化学习，无需模拟原始音频即可实现快速触觉反馈驱动的灵巧操作策略学习与迁移。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact events that are fast, local, and often visually occluded. Piezoelectric microphones offer a compact and high-bandwidth way to sense these interactions, but the resulting vibro-acoustic signals are difficult to simulate faithfully enough for end-to-end sim-to-real policy learning on dexterous robot hands. We propose VibeAct, a framework that bridges real vibrotactile sensing and simulation-based reinforcement learning through a shared physical representation of contact and slip. In the real world, we embed piezoelectric microphones into a dexterous robot hand and collect vibro-acoustic data through teleoperation, then replay the recordings in a calibrated digital clone to automatically label per-finger contact and slip. A tactile estimator learns to predict contact and slip from real microphone waveforms, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation on the same representation computed directly from simulated contacts. This decoupling lets policies exploit rapid tactile feedback without simulating raw audio. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning regrasping, in-hand reorientation, and insertion, VibeAct consistently outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline in simulation, with the largest gains on tasks requiring sustained reactive control, where the continuous slip-magnitude channel proves the most informative observation. The learned policies transfer to a physical dexterous hand-arm platform, improving success rates on deployed tasks. Project videos and additional details are at https://vibeact.github.io/.

</details>


### [145] [Bridging Performance and Generalization in Reinforcement Learning for Agile Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27348)
*Jonathan Green,Jiaxu Xing,Nico Messikommer,Angel Romero,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无人机竞速强化学习策略的零样本泛化框架，通过结合基于学习进度的任务感知切换与物理启发的程序化赛道生成器，在不降低飞行速度的前提下，实现了7.4倍于现有方法的泛化性能提升，并在无显式状态估计的视觉端到端控制中取得了成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机竞速强化学习策略在特定环境中表现优异，但无法泛化到未见配置，在高速飞行中安全与性能紧密耦合，而现有提升泛化的方法以大幅牺牲飞行速度为代价。

Method: 提出联合框架：(1) 基于学习进度的任务感知切换机制；(2) 物理启发的程序化赛道生成器。无需测试时自适应即可产生快速且鲁棒的通用策略。

Result: 在真实世界多种未见赛道中实现强零样本性能，泛化能力较现有最优方法提升7.4倍，同时保持有竞争力的竞速速度；在无显式状态估计的视觉端到端控制中，所有先前方法均失败而本方法成功。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了敏捷飞行中零样本泛化的难题，在不牺牲速度的前提下大幅提升了泛化能力，并在最具挑战性的视觉端到端场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.

</details>


### [146] [Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27353)
*Jiaxu Xing,Zhiyuan Zhu,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yifan Zhai,Rudolf Reiter,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种持续学习框架，通过结合物理先验与神经残差的隐条件动态模型，使机器人能在部署中识别并适应重复出现的环境变化（如风力变化），性能显著优于在线残差重拟合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人部署中动态条件（风、负载、电池等）持续变化，而现有学习控制器训练后即固定，无法利用部署经验进一步优化策略，导致在非固定动态下性能受限。

Method: 1) 构建条件感知动态模型：解析物理先验+神经残差；2) 循环编码器从近期交互推断隐藏条件；3) 基于可微仿真和多样化的学习动态采样进行策略学习；4) 部署时用在线推断的条件替代采样条件，通过识别而非残差重拟合恢复重复动态。

Result: 四旋翼无人机在变化风力下轨迹跟踪，约1秒内恢复重复干扰（比在线残差重拟合快5倍），大扰动悬停误差降低65.7%，跟踪误差降低53.3%。

Conclusion: 提出框架通过条件识别机制显著提升了机器人在未观测干扰下的策略性能，验证了利用部署经验进行持续学习的有效性。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>


### [147] [RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27355)
*Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Ge Ma,Youran Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RouterVLA方法，通过在预部署评估中重用记录数据来监督策略选择，使用结果不相交的交叉拟合方法评估专家策略，在34,752条LIBERO-Plus记录上，透明探针成功规则将留出成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp），并发现委托（commissioning）承载路由价值，而额外的标量评分器容量并不创造价值，且重用评分试验会膨胀收益测量1.87倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究能否重用预部署评估的试运行记录（rollouts）来监督策略选择过程，即利用已有的测试数据来更有效地选择最优策略，避免额外的评估开销。

Method: 提出RouterVLA框架，采用结果不相交的交叉拟合（outcome-disjoint cross-fitting）：用记录的探针数据构建每个冻结专家的档案（profile），然后用独立的试运行（trial）来评分被选中的专家，确保评分数据不进入该专家的档案。使用透明探针成功规则和可学习的评分器进行对比实验。

Result: 在34,752条LIBERO-Plus rollout记录上，透明探针成功规则将留出成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp）。可学习的评分器与透明规则在统计上无显著差异。重用已评分试验会膨胀收益测量1.87倍。

Conclusion: 委托（commissioning）承载了路由价值，而额外的标量评分器容量并不能创造路由价值。可信的账本式路由需要结果分离（outcome separation）。模型规模提升改善了单个策略，而委托感知的路由（commissioning-aware routing）提升了由这些策略构建的系统整体性能。

Abstract: We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.

</details>


### [148] [World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27374)
*Manish Kumar Govind,Dominick Reilly,Smit Patel,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出REGEN框架，利用世界行动模型生成伪重放轨迹，在无需存储原始人类演示的情况下实现机器人持续模仿学习，将灾难性遗忘降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人在持续学习新任务时，因无法存储原始人类演示数据而导致的灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 基于世界行动模型(WAM)的生成能力，提出递归生成重放(REGEN)框架，通过递归查询WAM合成仅依赖先前任务指令和当前任务观测的伪重放轨迹，使机器人策略能够复现先前学习过的任务。

Result: 在仿真和真实操作实验中，REGEN相比顺序微调减少了高达50%的灾难性遗忘，性能接近需要真实重放数据的特权经验重放方法。

Conclusion: WAM作为无需存储演示的持续机器人学习的基础方法具有广阔前景；长程视觉退化和动作-观测不一致性是限制生成重放的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>


### [149] [Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27375)
*Arthur Allshire,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Ritvik Singh,Adam Rashid,Hongsuk Choi,David McAllister,Justin Yu,Yiyuan Chen,Huang Huang,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Rocky Duan,Phillip Isola,Jitendra Malik,Fred Shentu,Guanya Shi,Philipp Wu,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABC是一个完全开源的行为克隆操作框架，包含最大开源遥操作数据集ABC-130K（3500小时/13万+ episodes/195个任务），并开源了硬件、训练基础设施和仿真管线，提供了400小时仿真数据及联合训练方案，验证了DiT和VLA架构在真实世界任务（如折盒、取信用卡）中的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前行为克隆研究中缺乏大规模、多样化且完全开源的数据集和可复现的工具链，导致研究者之间资源不对等、实验难以公平比较。作者希望通过开源全套方案降低门槛，让社区在统一基准上共同推进行为克隆研究。

Method: 1) 构建ABC-130K数据集：3500小时/130K episodes/195任务的遥操作数据；2) 开源硬件平台、训练基础设施和仿真管线；3) 额外收集400小时仿真遥操作数据并提供联合训练方案；4) 在仿真和真实世界中对DiT和VLA架构进行消融实验和比较。

Result: 训练出的策略能够成功执行精细操作任务，如折纸盒和从钱包中取出信用卡。仿真与真实世界评估具有相关性，可作为可靠代理指标。

Conclusion: 通过提供可复现的全套工具包，使研究者处于平等地位，为社区共同学习行为克隆奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Rendering Novel Views of MRI Using 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26236)
*Robin Y. Park,Mark C. Eid,Rhydian Windsor,Amir Jamaludin,Ana I. L. Namburete,João F. Henriques,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文通过3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splatting）从稀疏各向异性MRI重建体积，并采样与临床相关解剖平面对齐的新视图，从而改进脊柱MRI的放射学分级，实验证明该方法在椎管狭窄分级上优于原始扫描和体素插值重采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善脊柱MRI中因原始扫描平面与目标解剖结构对齐不佳导致放射学分级不准确的问题。

Method: 采用3D高斯泼溅从稀疏各向异性MRI进行体积重建，然后采样和渲染与目标解剖结构对齐的最优诊断平面，使用重采样图像预测局部椎管狭窄的序数严重度分级。

Result: 在多种椎管狭窄条件下，基于3D高斯泼溅重采样的分级准确度均优于原始扫描和体素插值重采样方法。

Conclusion: 3D高斯泼溅重采样能有效改善脊柱MRI的放射学分级性能，尤其适用于原始图像中目标解剖结构在平面内不完整的情况。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to improve radiological gradings measured on MRIs of spines, by resampling scans so that the new view planes are better aligned with the target anatomy than the original sparse images. To this end, we adapt 3D Gaussian Splatting to form a volumetric reconstruction starting from sparse anisotropic MRIs, and imaging planes aligned with the anatomy relevant for clinical evaluation are then sampled and rendered. The novel view plane is optimal for diagnostic radiological grading of the target anatomy, whereas the original MRI is not. The resampled scans are then used to predict ordinal severity grades of localised stenosis conditions in spinal MRIs. We compare our method against Voxel Interpolation resampling, which takes the average of inverse-distance weighted nearest neighbour intensities for each target coordinate. Experiments show that across all stenosis conditions, resampled scans using Gaussian Splatting produce more accurate stenosis gradings compared to the raw scans which do not include the complete anatomy in-plane, as well as images resampled using Voxel Interpolation.

</details>


### [151] [An Evaluation of ABR Switching for Time-Shifted Clients in MoQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26368)
*Abanisenioluwa Orojo,Tanvir Redoy,Samira Afzal,Andrew C. Freeman*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究了SWITCH规范下Media over QUIC (MoQ)的自适应比特率(ABR)算法性能，通过Mininet仿真验证了标准ABR算法可直接应用于时移播放且能显著提升吞吐量，并发现订阅者在经历缓冲后整体吞吐量可能增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Media over QUIC (MoQ)默认质量切换语义在网络拥塞时可能导致播放间隙（卡顿），而正在制定的SWITCH规范旨在优化MoQ的码率自适应，因此需要系统评估SWITCH风格ABR算法的性能表现。

Method: 在Mininet模拟拓扑中，针对直播和时移两种播放场景，对SWITCH风格的ABR算法进行性能表征实验，对比标准ABR算法在两种场景下的表现。

Result: 标准ABR算法无需修改即可直接应用于时移播放，且吞吐量显著提升；订阅者在经历重缓冲场景后整体吞吐量可能增加。

Conclusion: SWITCH风格的ABR在MoQ中表现良好，标准ABR可直接迁移至时移场景，但MoQ ABR切换仍有进一步优化的空间。

Abstract: Media over QUIC enables ultra low latency video streaming over QUIC, but its default quality-switching semantics risk introducing playback gaps during periods of network congestion. The in-progress SWITCH specification for MOQ Transport aims to streamline rate adaptation for MoQ. In this work, we characterize the performance of SWITCH-style Adaptive Bitrate (ABR) for both live and time-shifted clients in a Mininet simulated topology. We validate that standard ABR algorithms can be directly applied to time-shifted playback without modification, yielding substantially higher throughput. We demonstrate that a subscriber can experience increased overall throughput after a rebuffering scenario, and we identify focal points for further optimizations of MoQ ABR switching.

</details>


### [152] [Design Guidelines for In-line X-ray Inspection in Advanced Packaging Technology: A CoWoS Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26430)
*Katayoon Yahyaei,M Shafkat M Khan,Guillermo Cadima,Himanandhan Reddy Kottur,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI集成实验设计（DoE）的框架，用于在先进封装（如CoWoS）设计阶段优化X射线兼容性，通过系统分析设计参数和材料特性，生成设计指南以提高检测精度和缺陷检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进封装技术（2.5D/3D集成）带来了复杂的三维结构，传统X射线检测面临材料密度相似、噪声散射等兼容性问题，亟需在设计阶段优化X射线兼容性以提升无损检测效果。

Method: 提出AI集成实验设计（DoE）框架，通过系统分析设计参数和材料属性，利用AI预测结果并优化过程，生成设计指南以改善X射线成像质量。

Result: 在CoWoS封装案例中，该框架能够系统性地生成设计指南，提升X射线图像质量和缺陷检测能力。

Conclusion: 该指南可显著提高检测精度和可靠性，降低生产成本，支持先进半导体技术的效率和可扩展性。

Abstract: The shift towards advanced packaging technologies, including 2.5D and 3D integration, addresses the limitations of traditional methods while meeting increasing demands for performance, miniaturization, and efficiency. These methods enhance functionality and support heterogeneous integration but also introduce metrology challenges due to complex, three-dimensional structures. X-ray imaging, crucial for nondestructive inspection, faces compatibility issues such as material density similarities and noise scattering. To address these challenges, we propose a framework based on AI-integrated Design of Experiment (DoE) to develop design guidelines to optimize X-ray compatibility during the design stage. This framework, demonstrated through a case study on Chip-on-Wafer-on-Substrate (CoWoS) packaging, systematically analyzes design parameters and material properties to develop guidelines for improved inspection accuracy. Our method integrates AI to predict outcomes and optimize processes, ensuring high-quality X-ray images and enhancing defect detection. Implementing these guidelines can significantly improve inspection accuracy and reliability, reducing production costs and supporting the efficiency and scalability of advanced semiconductor technologies.

</details>


### [153] [Revealing Mammographic Phenotypes in Deep Learning Breast Cancer Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26431)
*Ruiyu Jia,Yanqi Xu,Yuxuan Chen,Yiqiu Shen,Laura Heacock*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 通过聚类Mirai模型提取的影像patch嵌入，识别与5年乳腺癌风险相关的重复性影像表型，发现高风险表型包含复杂组织结构和伪影，并与年龄、BI-RADS密度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于乳腺X光片的深度学习模型虽能提升乳腺癌风险预测，但模型学习的影像模式尚未被充分探索。传统可解释性方法依赖单张图像的显著性图，无法在大规模患者队列中识别重复出现的乳腺影像表型。

Method: 对预训练Mirai模型提取的patch嵌入进行聚类，从大量患者中分离出与5年癌症风险相关的重复性影像表型。

Result: 风险增加的表型包含复杂结构（如致密组织、微钙化）以及捷径伪影（如夹子标记）。这些表型与高龄和更高的BI-RADS密度强相关。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将组织模式与AI风险评分联系起来，揭示临床特征及潜在的模型混杂因素。

Abstract: Mammogram-based deep learning models have improved breast cancer risk prediction, but the learned imaging patterns remain underexplored. Existing interpretability methods rely on single-image saliency maps, failing to identify recurring mammographic phenotypes across large patient cohorts. By clustering patch embeddings from a pre-trained model, Mirai, we isolate recurring phenotypes linked to 5-year cancer risk. Analyses show risk-increasing phenotypes capture complex structures (e.g., dense tissue, microcalcifications) and shortcut artifacts (e.g., clips). These phenotypes correlate strongly with older age and higher BI-RADS density. Our framework connects tissue patterns to AI risk scores, revealing clinical signatures and potential latent model confounders.

</details>


### [154] [MLFFM-SegDiff: A Multi-Level Feature Fusion Diffusion Model for Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26712)
*Jingjun Gu,Chaojie Shen,Yifeng Cao,Wei Zhang,Yiliu Li,Aobo Fan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MLFFM-SegDiff，一种多级特征融合扩散模型用于皮肤病变分割。通过双路径 U-Net 编码器、多级特征融合模块（MLFFM）和边界敏感损失函数，解决了扩散方法中跨层级特征交互不足和边界细节恢复不充分的问题。在 ISIC2018、PH2 和 HAM10000 数据集上，该方法在 Accuracy、F1-score、Jaccard index、Recall 和 Dice 指标上均优于现有代表方法，平均 Jaccard 指数达 0.8546，Dice 系数达 0.9207。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤镜图像存在边界模糊、对比度低、形状变化大以及毛发阴影等伪影干扰，现有扩散分割方法在跨层级特征交互和边界细节恢复方面仍存在不足。

Method: 基于扩散框架，引入双路径 U-Net 编码器以增强噪声掩膜特征与皮肤镜图像特征间的交互；设计多级特征融合模块（MLFFM），通过注意力、尺度对齐和自适应跨层级融合改进跳跃连接；采用边界敏感损失函数，使解码器联合利用浅层边界线索和深层语义表征以提升掩膜重建质量。

Result: 在 ISIC2018、PH2 和 HAM10000 三个数据集上，MLFFM-SegDiff 在 Accuracy、F1-score、Jaccard index、Recall 和 Dice 指标上均优于 DermoSegDiff、U-Net、SwinUNETR 等方法，平均 Jaccard 指数为 0.8546，Dice 系数为 0.9207。

Conclusion: 提出的多级特征融合策略能有效提升皮肤病变分割性能，代码将在论文发表后开源。

Abstract: Skin lesion segmentation is a key task in computer-aided dermatological diagnosis, where accuracy directly impacts downstream analysis and disease classification. However, dermoscopic images are challenging due to blurred boundaries, low contrast, large shape variations, and artifacts such as hair and shadows. Recently, diffusion models have shown strong performance in medical image segmentation thanks to their progressive denoising and distribution modeling capabilities. Nevertheless, existing diffusion-based methods still suffer from limited cross-level feature interaction and insufficient boundary detail recovery.
  To address these issues, we propose MLFFM-SegDiff, a multi-level feature fusion diffusion model for skin lesion segmentation. Built on a diffusion framework, the method introduces a dual-path U-Net encoder, a Multi-Level Feature Fusion Module (MLFFM), and a boundary-sensitive loss function. The dual-path encoder enhances interaction between noisy mask features and dermoscopic image features. MLFFM improves skip connections via attention, scale alignment, and adaptive cross-level fusion. These designs enable the decoder to jointly leverage shallow boundary cues and deep semantic representations, improving mask reconstruction quality.
  Experiments on ISIC2018, PH2, and HAM10000 demonstrate that MLFFM-SegDiff outperforms representative methods including DermoSegDiff, U-Net, and SwinUNETR across Accuracy, F1-score, Jaccard index, Recall, and Dice. In particular, it achieves an average Jaccard index of 0.8546 and Dice coefficient of 0.9207. These results validate the effectiveness of the proposed multi-level feature fusion strategy for improving lesion segmentation performance. The code will be released at https://github.com/Qacket/MLFFM-SegDiff.git after publication.

</details>


### [155] [Dual-Prior Guided Null-Space Learning with Mixture-of-Splines for Arbitrary Medical Slice Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26716)
*Haofei Song,Siyuan Xu,Xintian Mao,Shaojie Guo,Qingli Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: DP-NSL框架将任意切片超分辨率重构转化为受约束的恢复过程，通过测量一致性投影确保重构与原始采集切片零误差，同时在零空间内使用混合样条模块实现内容感知的几何连续性先验，在CT和MRI基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任意切片超分辨率方法将病态逆问题视为无约束残差回归，容易产生解剖学上不合理的伪影或改变原始观测数据，缺乏对测量一致性和几何连续性的约束。

Method: 提出Dual-Prior Null-space Learning (DP-NSL)框架：(1) 测量一致性投影(MCP)通过正交投影确保重构严格再现采集切片，将学习细节限制在不可观测零空间；(2) 混合样条模块(MoS)动态组合不同解析阶数的B样条专家，实现内容感知的几何连续性先验；(3) 局部空间一致性解码器(LSCD)注入局部归纳偏置促进空间连贯性。

Result: 在三个CT和一个MRI基准数据集上，DP-NSL优于现有方法，同时严格保持测量一致性。

Conclusion: 通过将无约束回归重构为受双先验约束的恢复问题，DP-NSL有效解决了解剖学伪影和观测数据改变问题，为医学图像各向同性超分辨率提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Arbitrary slice super-resolution reconstructs isotropic volumes from anisotropic clinical acquisitions by synthesizing intermediate slices at arbitrary scales. However, treating this ill-posed inverse problem as unconstrained residual-based regression risks hallucinating anatomically implausible structures or altering the originally observed data. To address both concerns, this paper presents the Dual-Prior Null-space Learning (DP-NSL) framework, which reformulates the task as a constrained recovery process guided by two complementary priors. A Measurement-Consistent Projection (MCP) enforces a Deterministic Observation Prior: the reconstruction undergoes an exact orthogonal projection that reproduces every acquired slice with zero error, confining all learned details to the unobservable null space. Within this null space, a Mixture-of-Splines (MoS) module imposes a Geometric Continuity Prior by dynamically mixing B-spline experts of different analytic orders, allowing each anatomical region to be modeled with a content-aware level of continuity. To promote spatial coherence, a Local Spatial Consistency Decoder (LSCD) further injects local inductive bias. Experiments on three CT and one MRI benchmark show that DP-NSL outperforms existing approaches while strictly preserving measurement consistency. Code is available at https://github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Medical-Image-Reconstruction.

</details>


### [156] [Enabling self-supervised learned primal dual with Noise2Inverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26991)
*Antti Sällinen,Siiri Rautio,Santeri Kaupinmäki,Andreas Hauptmann*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Noise2Inverse Learned Primal-Dual (N2I-LPD) 方法，将自监督 Noise2Inverse 框架与 Learned Primal-Dual 算法结合，无需真实标签即可训练学习型迭代重建算子，在低剂量和稀疏角 CT 重建中取得优于传统方法和 U-Net 的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型 CT 重建方法（如 Learned Primal-Dual）依赖有监督训练，需要真实标签数据，而在实际低剂量和稀疏角 CT 场景中，真实标签往往难以获取。

Method: 将 Noise2Inverse 自监督框架扩展到 Learned Primal-Dual 算法中，利用不同角度测量数据间噪声的统计独立性，无需地面真值图像即可训练迭代重建算子。

Result: N2I-LPD 相比经典重建方法和基于 U-Net 的 Noise2Inverse 方法，取得了更优的重建质量。

Conclusion: 将学习型重建算子与自监督训练策略相结合，在缺乏真实标签的实际 CT 成像场景中具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography reconstruction is an ill-posed inverse problem, particularly in low-dose and sparse-angle settings where measurements are noisy and incomplete. While learned reconstruction methods such as the Learned Primal-Dual algorithm achieve strong performance, they typically rely on supervised training with access to ground-truth data, which is often unavailable in practice.
  In this work, we propose a self-supervised reconstruction method by extending the Noise2Inverse framework to the Learned Primal-Dual algorithm. The resulting approach, called Noise2Inverse Learned Primal-Dual (N2I-LPD), enables training of a learned iterative reconstruction operator without ground-truth images by exploiting the statistical independence of noise in distinct measurements with respect to angular rotation of the CT-scan.
  We compare the proposed method with classical reconstruction methods, as well as neural network-based approaches such as a U-Net trained within the same N2I framework. The results demonstrate that N2I-LPD achieves improved reconstruction quality, highlighting the potential of combining learned reconstruction operators with self-supervised training strategies for practical CT imaging scenarios where ground-truth data is unavailable.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代数据生成管道，通过隔离与行为呈线性级联的特征样本，而非简单二元对比样本，来更好地解耦和操控语言模型中的谄媚行为。该方法在检测、评分和引导方面优于或持平基线方法，且计算成本更低、可解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活引导方法依赖二元对比样本来解释和控制模型行为，但二元样本难以充分解耦复杂行为背后的特征，导致特征检测不可靠、引导效果受限。作者旨在通过更精细的级联样本设计，提升特征解耦能力和行为操控效果。

Method: 提出迭代数据生成管道，生成展示行为程度变化的级联样本（而非简单二元对比），从中提取与行为线性相关的级联线性特征。以语言模型的谄媚行为为案例，通过级联样本发现的特征形成线性可分子空间，用于检测、确定性评分和鲁棒引导。

Result: 级联样本发现的谄媚特征形成线性可分子空间，能够选择更清晰对应目标行为的模型激活。在检测、确定性评分和鲁棒引导任务中，该方法匹配或优于LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，同时计算需求更低，可解释性保障更强。

Conclusion: 超越简单二元对比，采用展示行为程度变化的级联样本来隔离级联线性特征，能有效提升特征解耦和行为操控效果，为模型可解释性和可控性提供更优方案。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [158] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出当基准测试准确率饱和后，不应直接替换，而应从构造效度、泛化能力、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架相对重要性、人机协作提升等六个维度继续评估。以CORE-Bench Hard为例，展示了这些维度如何在准确率饱和后仍提供有意义的洞察，并引入了改进版本CORE-Bench v1.1、OOD测试集以及人机协作的随机实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估范式过度聚焦于准确率这一单一指标，当基准测试准确率饱和后被直接废弃，忽视了其他关键性能维度。作者希望提出一种更严格的替代评估范式，通过多维指标持续衡量智能体表现。

Method: 以CORE-Bench Hard（科学代码可重复性基准）为案例，分析其构造效度问题（如捷径），提出改进版CORE-Bench v1.1和OOD测试集；测量准确率饱和后的效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架贡献；开展小规模随机实验测量人机协作带来的提升。

Result: 发现了CORE-Bench Hard中难以预见的构造效度威胁；CORE-Bench v1.1在准确率饱和后仍可用于衡量效率、可靠性、模型性能和脚手架性能；人机协作带来约两倍的统计显著加速，且因五分之一纯人工作业超时而可能被低估。

Conclusion: 多维评估范式比单纯追求准确率更严格、更有信息量，为基准测试设计提供了更优的替代方案。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [159] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调模型中的顺从人格方向会门控拒绝方向，拒绝并非独立机制，而是依赖于人格表达。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，顺从人格干预可显著抑制拒绝率（Llama从97%降至2%），且拒绝门控发生在后期层表达阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究分别识别了激活空间中的拒绝方向和人格特质方向，但未探究二者之间的交互机制。本文旨在揭示顺从人格与拒绝行为之间的门控关系，挑战将拒绝视为独立方向的传统观点。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct两个模型中，分别提取顺从模型人格方向和拒绝方向，通过干预实验：1) 顺从人格方向干预抑制拒绝；2) 在后期层重新引入拒绝方向部分恢复拒绝；3) 在后期层窗口投影掉人格方向可恢复到基线水平。

Result: 顺从人格方向干预使Llama的拒绝率从97%降至2%；后期层重新引入拒绝方向可部分恢复拒绝，但早期层无效；后期层投影掉人格方向可恢复基线拒绝水平，而投影随机方向则不能。

Conclusion: 拒绝并非独立机制，而是被顺从人格在后期层表达阶段门控。将拒绝视为单一孤立方向会忽略其对人格的依赖，这对理解模型安全行为机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [160] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlgoEvolve，一个基于LLM的进化框架，用于在算法交易领域自动生成、评估和迭代改进可执行的交易策略。系统展现出涌现性的自适应逻辑，并引入元进化外循环来优化引导内循环程序合成的提示，从而在复杂环境中实现持续的程序合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM语义变异算子主要用于静态编码基准测试，尚未在算法交易这一噪声大、非平稳、高度不连续的复杂领域得到充分探索。作者希望将LLM驱动的进化范式扩展到这一更具挑战性的实际应用场景。

Method: 提出AlgoEvolve框架，包含两个循环：(1) 内循环：LLM作为语义变异算子，生成、评估并迭代改进以Python代码表达的交易策略；(2) 外循环（元进化）：对指导内循环程序合成的提示进行进化优化，自动发现更好的搜索启发式，平衡探索与利用并减少零交易失败。

Result: 系统展现出涌现性的制度自适应策略逻辑，能够自主转换交易规则。元进化外循环发现的搜索启发式一致优于初始人工设计的指令，有效平衡了探索与利用，并减少了零交易失败。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义进化是复杂环境中持续程序合成的一种可行方法，为算法交易等动态领域的自动策略发现提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [161] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的治理话语分析流水线，结合自动化标注、神经主题建模和多层网络分析，对两个AI代理互操作性标准（ERC-8004与Google A2A）的治理结构进行大规模比较研究，发现开放治理虽能促进主题趋同，但两类标准在参与不平等性和社区碎片化程度上并无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理协议日益增多，但其互操作性标准的治理结构在实证层面尚未得到充分研究，亟需大规模、系统性的分析方法来理解其中的社会技术权力结构。

Method: 构建了基于LLM的比较分析流水线，集成了LLM辅助编码、神经主题建模和多层网络分析，对ERC-8004（无许可、链上）和Google A2A（企业主导）两个标准的4,323条治理参与记录进行分析。

Result: 治理形式影响主题聚焦方向，但两类标准在参与不平等性和社区碎片化程度上表现相当；在无许可治理环境下话语对齐更密集，表明开放治理可能促进更强的主题趋同。

Conclusion: LLM辅助方法可推进技术治理的实证研究，为设计更公平的自主AI标准提供启示。数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [162] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，将患者自述数据（Reddit、WebMD）与FDA官方不良事件报告系统进行溯源感知的整合，并通过本体词汇表（ATC-N、ICD-10、MedDRA）在Neo4j图中保持出处可追溯性，验证了患者生成数据与监管数据部分独立的安全信号价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神病药物信息在监管记录（权威但抽象）与患者叙述（贴近经验但未经验证）之间存在割裂，不当整合会放大恐惧、反安慰剂效应及用药不依从，亟需一种不混淆证据与轶事的溯源感知整合方法。

Method: 收集466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论及20年美国FDA不良事件报告系统数据（针对九种抗抑郁药）；构建基于大语言模型的实体识别pipeline（经医师标注基准测试）；采用Jaccard相似度计算平台间一致性；利用Neo4j知识图谱，以ATC-N、ICD-10、MedDRA为词汇体系，保留每条声明的出处信息。

Result: LLM实体识别在药物（F1=0.969）和疾病（F1=0.973）上达到最高分；Reddit与WebMD高度一致（Jaccard相似度最高0.905），但与FDA报告一致性较低；舍曲林的许多不良事件在社区来源中出现的时间比FDA记录早数百天。

Conclusion: 建立了来源感知整合作为更可审计的精神科药物信息路径，患者生成数据构成部分独立的安全信号，其效用与患者获益有待前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [163] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DD-Elo模型，将认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型（DDM）融入传统Elo评分系统，利用棋局中每一步的走法信息来捕捉技能波动，从而实现对棋手评分更快速、更精细的调整，同时保持与传统Elo系统的理论一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo评分系统仅依赖比赛胜负结果，忽略了棋局过程中的细粒度质量信息，导致评分更新存在响应延迟，无法快速反映棋手实际技能水平的实时变化。

Method: 将技能表达建模为决策过程，引入漂移扩散模型（DDM），整合棋局中每一步走法数据，从认知神经科学视角构建新的技能评估框架，并给出数学推导证明其与传统Elo系统的偏差有界。

Result: 大量实验表明，DD-Elo在适应技能变化的速度上优于传统Elo系统，且具备可解释性、高响应性和向后兼容性。

Conclusion: DD-Elo为国际象棋评分生态系统提供了一种可解释、高响应且向后兼容的新型解决方案。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [164] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自主AI代理的计算治理模型，该模型通过独立见证、加密绑定的前提条件和确定性策略来约束高风险行动的执行权限，同时保留代理的完全自主规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理可能开始执行不可逆的高风险行动（如临床处方、软件部署），而人类机构在治理强大自主行动者时并非监控其推理过程，而是要求在关键行动点提供独立可验证的证据。作者希望将这种制度模式形式化为AI代理系统的计算治理模型。

Method: 提出计算治理模型：代理保留规划和推理的完全自主权，但对指定的高风险行动不拥有执行权限；执行依赖于每个由独立权威来源证明的前提条件、加密绑定到声明的意图，并由确定性策略评估；决策记录在可防篡改的日志中，可供独立重新验证。提供了概念验证实现。

Result: 提出了一个完整的计算治理框架，并通过软件部署和临床处方的示例进行了说明，同时提供了概念验证实现。

Conclusion: 该模型能够在保留AI代理自主规划能力的同时，通过独立见证和加密机制对高风险行动施加有效的执行控制，为AI安全治理提供了一种可行的制度化方案。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [165] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COrigami，一个端到端的AI驱动管道，能够从自然语言生成折纸折痕图案，通过结合几何约束优化和自主美学评估循环，实现数学严谨的人机协同创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在可验证解的问题上取得了显著成功，但在生成同时满足严格几何约束和主观视觉美学的物理艺术作品方面仍面临挑战。本文旨在解决计算折纸这一数学刚性环境中的设计难题。

Method: 提出COrigami管道，包括：生成语义线条图、计算基础排布、求解可平折的折痕图案、塑形平折折痕图案，以及使用强化学习驱动的自主美学评估循环进行模型优化。

Result: 该系统作为高效的协作助手，能生成结构化的设计起点供人类艺术家进一步扩展和塑形，展示了AI系统如何满足多目标物理约束以实现可靠的、数学基础的协同创造力。

Conclusion: 通过将算法优化与自主美学评判相结合，该工作证明了AI系统能够满足多目标物理约束，实现数学上严谨的人机协同创作。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [166] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出验证比生成更难的逆向直觉，系统分析了验证信号的三维挑战（可扩展性、忠实性、鲁棒性），并通过四种奖励构造的实验证明：验证需与生成器协同进化，固定奖励函数在能力增长下会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直觉认为验证比生成容易，但当前编码智能体时代，生成能力因基础模型和工程化提升而变得廉价，验证反而成为瓶颈。关键在于：任何验证器都只是人类意图的代理，而意图本身存在天然的不完备性，且在优化过程中代理与意图的差距会被放大（奖励黑客、信号饱和）。

Method: 从三个维度（可扩展性、忠实性、鲁棒性）刻画验证信号质量，并构建四种奖励方案：测试验证器（通用编码）、评分模板验证器（前端任务）、用户作为验证器（真实智能体任务）、自动化智能体验证器（长时域任务）。在不同任务类型和能力水平下进行实验分析。

Result: 针对性验证设计能有效抑制奖励黑客行为，提升任务完成质量，在多个内部和公开基准上取得显著收益。

Conclusion: 核心结论：没有固定的奖励函数能够在策略能力持续增长时始终保持有效；验证必须与生成器共同进化。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [167] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出针对能源市场分析领域的大语言模型智能体基准评估，包含243个专家级问题、多领域工具套件和多维度评估协议，填补了能源领域动态推理评估的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准覆盖了金融、编程、法律和药物发现等领域，但能源领域评估仅限于静态知识回忆，而该领域需要实时数据检索、专业监管和市场知识以及多步定量推理，存在关键评估空白。

Method: 构建了包含243个专家精选问题的评估环境，涵盖市场数据检索分析、知识检索解释、高级定量建模与决策分析三类任务；配置了包括实时电力市场API、监管文档搜索、资费数据库、资产优化模型和RAG在内的工具套件；采用多维度评估协议，从方法正确性、答案准确度、属性对齐度和来源有效性四个维度评分，并按类别路由匹配评分标准。

Result: 对闭源和开源大语言模型进行了比较分析，揭示了模型能力与领域工具在专业场景中的交互机制，并公开了关键工件以支持可复现性。

Conclusion: 能源领域需要超越静态知识回忆的动态评估范式，工具增强型智能体在实时数据检索、专业知识推理和多步定量分析方面具有潜力，本文工作为后续研究提供了基准和基础设施。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [168] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）评估基准的不足，集中在孤立任务上，缺乏对跨模态信息整合能力的评测，并识别了四个关键评估缺口：时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs能力快速提升，但评估方法滞后，现有基准局限于孤立任务，无法揭示模型是否真正整合跨模态信息，因此需要系统审视评估现状并识别评测缺口。

Method: 通过审查当前MLLMs评估手段，回顾现有基准分类法，系统性地识别评估体系中缺失的关键维度。

Result: 识别出四个评估缺口：时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性及选择性注意。

Conclusion: 填补这些评估缺口对于衡量多模态智能的真实进展和揭示能力边界至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [169] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenFinGym是一个统一的量化金融智能体开发环境，覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等多种任务，提供自动化任务构建、容器化运行时、纸面交易引擎等功能，支持SFT和RL后训练集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在量化金融领域的评估分散在孤立任务中，忽略了金融工作流的跨阶段依赖性（预测、策略构建、风险管理、交易等），导致高估智能体能力且无法揭示其在泛化、真实市场交互和金融意义决策方面的缺陷。

Method: 构建统一gym环境，包含：(1)自动化任务构建流水线（将量化金融论文转为可执行任务包）；(2)容器化运行时与主机端验证器服务（支持可扩展智能体部署，防止训练-测试泄漏）；(3)低延迟数据流设计的纸面交易引擎；(4)长周期和事件市场预测的延迟解析支持；(5)SFT和RL后训练集成。

Result: 提出了一个覆盖多任务的统一平台，解决了现有单任务评估的局限性，支持更全面的量化金融智能体能力评估。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym通过统一的执行和验证接口，为量化金融智能体开发提供了更全面、可扩展且金融意义更强的评估框架。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [170] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了提示组合代理系统中的组成性行为泄漏（CBL）问题，即编辑一个提示模块会静默改变其他模块的行为。通过部署的job-evaluation agent实验，发现内容通道会产生可检测的配对效应，虽不翻转推荐但会在大规模决策中累积。CBL与已知的代理故障轴正交，作者提供了操作定义、可复用协议、可证伪预测集和系统类特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示组合代理系统存在一个反复出现的故障模式：编辑一个提示模块会静默改变其他模块的行为，尽管它们之间没有共享变量或可执行依赖。这种干扰机制尚未被正式定义和系统研究。

Method: 使用三通道协议（volume、content、form）扰动非焦点模块，在部署的job-evaluation agent（Claude Sonnet 4.6，144次试验）上探测CBL。通过Cohen's d和bootstrap 95%置信区间量化效应大小。

Result: 只有内容通道产生可检测的配对效应（Cohen's d = 0.63，bootstrap 95% CI排除零）；没有推荐翻转——处于亚阈值状态，对标准QA不可见但在数千次决策中会累积。

Conclusion: CBL与已知的代理故障轴（对抗注入、认知退化、多代理故障传播、隐私泄漏）正交。跨模块干扰测量应成为提示组合代理评估的必要要求。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [171] [Accelerating Returns and the Qualitative Engine for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26359)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，虽然库兹韦尔的加速回报假说有助于解释量化能力的指数级增长，但它并未解决科学发现的核心问题——即在当前框架结构性不足时进行定性推理以提出概念突破的能力。文章以ARC-AGI-3基准测试中人类与AI的巨大差距为例，提出定性引擎科学（QES）作为对这一缺失能力的回应，强调科学发现过程本身是一种值得保存和传承的人类智慧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清库兹韦尔加速回报假说的适用范围与局限性：尽管该假说解释了许多技术领域的指数级进步，但它无法解释或解决科学发现中最关键的瓶颈——定性概念性突破的生成机制。作者试图论证加速回报主要适用于执行性能力，而非真正的科学发现能力。

Method: 论文采用理论分析方法，对库兹韦尔的加速回报假说给出简洁的数学解释，将其限定在执行性与基础设施能力的范畴内；随后通过ARC-AGI-3基准测试的实证结果（人类天花板vs.前沿AI低于1%）来区分量化加速与定性推理的差距；最后引入定性引擎科学（QES）框架作为对缺失能力的回应。

Result: 发现加速回报假说确实解释了量化能力的指数增长趋势，但无法解释科学发现中关键的概念性突破；ARC-AGI-3结果清晰表明了当前AI系统在灵活定性推理上与人脑的鸿沟仍然巨大；QES的提出并不依赖于AGI何时到来，而是基于科学发现过程本身作为人类智慧形式的内在价值。

Conclusion: 加速回报假说虽然有助于理解技术进步的量化侧，但科学发现的核心问题在于框架层面的定性判断与概念创新，而非单纯的速度提升。定性引擎科学（QES）作为弥补这一缺失能力的路径，其价值在于保存和组织科学发现过程中不可替代的人类智慧形式，而不仅仅是技术预测问题。

Abstract: Ray Kurzweil described a thesis of accelerating returns, which is the most influential narratives in discussions of technological progress. Its central claim is that advances in multiple technological fields, especially compute, artificial intelligence, brain science, and biotechnology, interact in such a way that progress becomes self-amplifying and approximately exponential. This paper gives a simple mathematical interpretation of that claim and then argues that, even if such acceleration is real, it does not by itself resolve the central problem of scientific discovery. The reason is that accelerating returns apply most naturally to executional and infrastructural capability, whereas genuine discovery often depends on a different capacity: qualitative reasoning about when a current framework is structurally inadequate and what conceptual move is needed next. Recent ARC-AGI-3 results sharpen this distinction: humans solve the benchmark at ceiling, whereas frontier AI systems remain below 1%, indicating that the gap between current AI and human flexible reasoning is still very large. At the same time, Demis Hassabis has emphasized that humans must retain their sense of meaning and what they choose to focus their lives on, a reminder that the future of AI is not only a technical forecast but also a question of what forms of human understanding are worth preserving and transmitting. This paper positions the Qualitative Engine for Science (QES) [3] as a response to that missing capacity. In this view, the Kurzweil theory helps explain why quantitative capability may accelerate, while QES addresses the central problem in scientific discovery that acceleration alone does not solve. Its value does not depend on when AGI arrives, but on the fact that the processes of scientific discovery themselves constitute a form of human wisdom worth preserving, organizing, and making accessible.

</details>


### [172] [Narration-of-Thought: Inference-Time Scaffolding for Defeasible Ethical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26366)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NoT（narration-of-thought）是一种无需额外训练的结构化思维链提示方法，通过五段式框架（主角、利益相关者、两步后果、不确定性、承诺）有效解决道德困境中的利益相关者坍缩和不确定性抑制问题，显著提升AI决策的透明度和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准思维链在道德困境中存在两种失败模式：利益相关者坍缩（最多只提及一方利益相关者）和不确定性抑制（在承诺行动前没有明确的未知或保留声明），导致决策过程不透明、不可审计。

Method: 提出NoT系统提示，将思维链结构化为五个部分：主角、利益相关者、两步后果、不确定性、承诺。无需额外训练、参数或微调。进一步采用文本梯度下降优化该框架，并扩展到多轮多利益相关者辩论协议。

Result: 在100个DailyDilemmas场景、4个生成器（来自3个供应商）上，NoT将利益相关者坍缩从31%降至1%以下，不确定性抑制从72%降至1-24%。保留Cliff's delta优势（利益相关者计数+0.79至+0.90，不确定性得分+0.65至+0.93）。扩展至五轮多利益相关者辩论后，将6%的僵局转化为95%完全共识。

Conclusion: NoT提供了一种可审计、结构化的决策框架，能够外化每个承诺所依据的利益相关者、后果和不确定性，为可靠的智能体部署提供了可审计的基底。

Abstract: Standard chain-of-thought on moral dilemmas exhibits two failure modes: stakeholder collapse (the trace names at most one party with a stake in the outcome) and uncertainty suppression (no explicit unknowns or hedges before committing to an action). We introduce narration-of-thought (NoT), a system prompt that structures chain-of-thought into five sections: protagonist, stakeholders, two-step consequences, uncertainty, then commitment. NoT adds no training, parameters, or fine-tuning. On 100 DailyDilemmas scenarios across four generators from three vendors, NoT cuts stakeholder collapse from up to 31% to under 1% and uncertainty suppression from up to 72% to 1-24% on every model. A matched-budget verbose-CoT control rules out token spend as the active ingredient; NoT retains Cliff's delta advantages of +0.79 to +0.90 on stakeholder count and +0.65 to +0.93 on uncertainty score for three of four generators, and a section ablation attributes each shift to its specific sub-instruction. Textual-gradient descent initialised at NoT improves the scaffold further; a cross-family training judge (different vendor from the generator) dominates an in-family one on every measured axis. Extended to a five-round multi-stakeholder debate protocol, the scaffold converts a 6% standoff into 95% full consensus on a calibration set and 100% combined convergence on a DailyDilemmas replication. The resulting traces externalise the stakeholders, consequences, and uncertainty grounding each commitment, providing an auditable substrate for dependable agentic deployment.

</details>


### [173] [Geometry-Aware MCTS for Extremal Problems in Combinatorial Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26399)
*Luoning Zhang,Xu Zhuang,Tianhao Wang,Nathan Kaplan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）框架，用于解决组合几何中的极值问题（如无三点共线问题）。该框架通过增量更新可行动作空间严格 enforcing 几何约束，将约束检查复杂度从 O(n³) 降低到 O(n²)，并利用几何对称性进行剪枝和批量状态转移以提升搜索效率。在五个问题中取得了新的最佳已知结果，Max-N3IL 问题在 n=82~119 的网格上找到了约 1.8n 大小的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合几何中的极值问题（如 n×n 网格中满足严格全局几何约束的点配置）面临计算爆炸问题：经典精确求解器受限于组合爆炸，强化学习和Transformer模型则难以处理稀疏奖励的"有效性悬崖"和二次方令牌消耗限制。

Method: 提出几何感知的MCTS框架：(1) 通过增量更新可行动作空间严格 enforcing 几何约束，针对共线点约束将检查复杂度从 O(n³) 降至 O(n²)；(2) 利用几何对称性进行规范剪枝以减少分支因子；(3) 使用对称批量状态转移加速搜索。

Result: 在考虑的六个问题中有五个取得了新的最佳已知计算结果。Max-N3IL 问题在 n=82~119 的网格上找到了约 1.8n 大小的配置；最小完全集问题在测试网格中找到了约 0.95n 大小的配置，提供了新的上界。

Conclusion: 几何感知MCTS是一个高度自适应的框架，能够有效发现组合几何中的新型配置。

Abstract: We study certain extremal problems in combinatorial geometry that ask about configurations of points in an $n \times n$ grid that satisfy strict, global geometric constraints. Classical exact solvers suffer from combinatorial explosion for these types of problems, and standard reinforcement learning and transformer-based models struggle with the sparse reward "validity cliff" and quadratic token-consumption limits. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose a Geometry-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) framework. Our approach strictly enforces geometric constraints through incremental updates to the feasible action space. For constraints about collections of collinear points, like those that occur in the classic No-Three-in-Line problem (Max-N3IL), this mechanism reduces the constraint checking complexity from $O(n^3)$ to $O(n^2)$. To improve search efficiency, we exploit geometric symmetries in two ways: canonical pruning during node expansion to reduce the branching factor, and symmetric batch transitions to accelerate the discovery of promising configurations. We perform extensive experiments and establish new best-known computational results on five out of six of the problems that we considered. Notably, for Max-N3IL we find configurations of size roughly $1.8 n$ for grids of size $82 \le n \le 119$. For the Smallest Complete Set problem, we find configurations of size roughly $0.95 n$, providing new upper bounds within the tested grids. This work establishes Geometry-Aware MCTS as a highly adaptable framework for discovering novel configurations in combinatorial geometry.

</details>


### [174] [When Agents Meet Electric Bus Fleet Operations: Pricing Behavior, Trade-offs, and Policy Implications in an Aggregator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26400)
*Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Ali Eslami,Luis Miranda-Moreno,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种智能体聚合框架，将基于优化的电动公交车调度模型与监督智能体（用于扰动检测、电价适应和调度评估）相结合，以协调电动公交车队的运行。基于真实车场的案例研究评估了日前和实时运行，结果表明该框架能够维护可行调度、选择性触发重新优化并提升充电与V2G灵活性利用，但也揭示了以利润为导向的配置可能从公共交通运营商身上提取价值的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动公交车队的运行需要在服务可靠性、电池荷电状态、充电桩可用性、电价、路线能量不确定性以及车辆到电网（V2G）机会之间持续协调，现有方法缺乏能够简化这一复杂决策环境的统一框架。

Method: 提出智能体聚合器框架，将基于优化的电动公交车调度模型（确保物理可行性）与监督智能体（负责扰动检测、电价适应和调度评估）耦合，通过智能体层解释运行条件变化、触发实时重新优化并定义灵活性价值在聚合器与公交运营商之间的分配。

Result: 在利润导向和运营导向两种协调模式下，通过考虑服务延迟、路线能量偏差、电价冲击及组合扰动评估表明：智能体聚合能维护可行调度、选择性触发重新优化并提升V2G灵活性利用；但以利润为导向的配置会从公交运营商处提取价值。

Conclusion: 智能体聚合器可有效管理电动公交车V2G运行，但在公共车队场景中需要透明的协调模式、可审计的电价设定机制以及明确的价值共享规则。

Abstract: Agentic systems are changing how complex operational tasks are coordinated, introducing a new paradigm for connecting heterogeneous data sources and automating processes. Electric bus fleets provide a relevant test case. Their operation requires continuous coordination between service reliability, battery state-of-charge, charger availability, electricity prices, route-energy uncertainty, and vehicle-to-grid (V2G) opportunities. This paper proposes an agentic aggregator framework that streamlines this decision environment by coupling an optimization-based electric bus scheduling model with supervisory agents for disturbance detection, tariff adaptation, and schedule evaluation. The optimization core enforces physical feasibility across routes, chargers, batteries, and V2G exchanges, while the agentic layer interprets changing operating conditions, triggers real-time re-optimization when needed, and defines how flexibility value is allocated between the aggregator and the public transport operator (PTO). A realistic depot case study evaluates day-ahead and real-time operations under profit-based and operation-based coordination modes, considering service delays, route-energy deviations, electricity price shocks, and combined disturbances. The results show that agentic aggregation can support adaptive fleet-grid coordination by maintaining feasible schedules, activating re-optimization selectively, and improving the use of charging and V2G flexibility. However, they also reveal a critical trade-off: the same agentic capability that reduces operational complexity can extract value from the PTO when configured around profit-oriented pricing. These findings suggest that agentic aggregators can become useful for managing electric bus V2G operations, but their deployment in public-fleet contexts requires transparent coordination modes, auditable tariff-setting, and explicit value-sharing rules.

</details>


### [175] [Unbiased Canonical Set-Valued Oracles Via Lattice Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26418)
*Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自指涉的"先知"AI方案：当AI预测未来事件概率时，其预测结果被学习并采取行动后会改变所预测的概率。传统的反事实方案会使答案在被告知后失效，本文则采用credal集（概率信念集）替代单点概率估计，并通过Knaster-Tarski不动点定理在封闭credal集的完备格上构造最小不动点，保证自洽性与非平凡性，且对非表演性问题退化为经典点估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非智能体"先知"AI在估计未来事件概率时面临自指涉问题：其答案一旦被学习并采取行动，会改变它被要求预测的概率本身。现有方案（如科学家AI计划倡导的反事实问题）要求答案被评估为"没有影响力"，但这会导致答案在被得知后即刻失效（因为前提条件不再成立）。因此需要一种自洽的替代方案。

Method: 采用credal集（封闭凸概率集）替代单点概率估计；利用Knaster-Tarski不动点定理在封闭credal集的完备格上定义保序算子，取其最小不动点作为规范答案；变体方案取包含所有自洽点估计的最小不动点。证明存在性、自洽性、非空性，以及该构造在非表演性问题下退化为经典点估计。对二元事件在自然凸包分解假设下，规范答案为区间。

Result: 证明了所构造最小不动点的存在性、自洽性和非空性；展示了该构造对非表演性问题退化为经典点答案；证明了对于二元事件，在自然凸包分解假设下，规范答案为区间。整个发展是纯格论框架的，并从二元事件B扩展到了任意随机变量X。

Conclusion: 本文通过格论不动点方法为自指涉预测问题提供了规范的credal集答案，解决了反事实方案中答案失效的问题。但留下了开放问题，包括区间刻画能否推广到一般随机变量情形。

Abstract: A non-agentic "oracle" AI that estimates probabilities of future events faces a self-reference problem: once its answer is learned and acted upon, it can change the very probability it was asked to report. One response, advocated for the Scientist AI programme, is to ask only counterfactual questions, evaluated as if the answer had no influence. We observe that such answers tend to become irrelevant the moment they are learned, precisely because their premise is then false. We therefore explore a self-referential alternative in which the oracle reports not a single probability but a credal set that is simultaneously unbiased and self-consistent with the consequences of being learned. The naive self-consistency requirement is satisfied by too many sets (including the useless answer $[0,1]$), so the problem is to single out a canonical, nontrivial member. We do so with the Knaster--Tarski fixed-point theorem on the complete lattice of closed credal sets, taking the least fixed point of a suitably defined isotone operator; a variant instead reports the least fixed point that contains every self-consistent point estimate. We prove existence, self-consistency, and nonemptiness, show that the construction collapses to the classical point answer for non-performative questions, and that for a binary event the canonical answer is, under a natural hull-factoring assumption, an interval. The development is purely lattice-theoretic and extends unchanged from a binary event $B$ to an arbitrary random variable $X$, with $P(B\mid A,C)$ replaced by the conditional law $\mathcal{L}(X\mid A,C)$. We close with open questions, including whether the interval characterization itself survives that generalization.

</details>


### [176] [Estimating Uncertainty in Classifier Performance with Applications to Large Language Models and Nested Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26422)
*Kylie Anglin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了社会科学文本分类场景下（小样本、低频构念、嵌套数据）性能指标的置信区间方法，发现Wald区间和基本百分位自助法准确率最低，而Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson及一种新颖的伪计数正则化自助法（特别适用于F1）表现更优。对于嵌套数据，需同时调整有效N和自由度；层次自助法在中等文本数时优于聚类自助法，但在极少文本时过于保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用文本分类来测量构念，但性能指标（如召回率、精确率）的置信区间报告不一致且常使用不适当的方法，特别是在标注数据集小或性能高的情况下，缺乏针对社会科学典型条件（小到中等样本量、低频构念、个体内嵌套文本）的系统评估。

Method: 通过模拟研究，在社会科学文本分类的典型条件下（小到中等样本量、低频构念、文本嵌套于个体内），比较了多种置信区间方法，包括Wald区间、基本百分位自助法、Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson区间以及提出的伪计数正则化自助法，并针对嵌套数据检验了层次自助法与聚类自助法。

Result: 默认方法（Wald区间和基本百分位自助法）覆盖精度最差，有时远低于名义95%水平。Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson区间以及伪计数正则化自助法准确性更高。嵌套数据中，需同时调整有效N和自由度；层次自助法在个体产生中等数量文本时优于聚类自助法，但个体仅产生少数文本时过于保守。

Conclusion: 本文为社会科学文本分类中的置信区间选取提供了具体指导，以提升机器学习应用的透明度，并鼓励在研究设计阶段更加关注验证样本量。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use text classification--supervised models or large language models--to measure constructs from natural language, providing metrics such as recall and precision as evidence of their validity. Yet, though these metrics are point estimates subject to sampling variation, measures of uncertainty are inconsistently reported alongside them. Further, when they are reported, they are often estimated with methods that are not appropriate when relevant labelled datasets are small or performance is high. To increase and improve confidence interval reporting in the field, this paper evaluates confidence interval methods for performance metrics under conditions typical of social science text classification: small to moderate sample sizes, infrequent constructs, and texts nested within individuals. Across simulations, default methods such as the Wald interval and the basic percentile bootstrap are the least accurate, with coverage sometimes far below the nominal 95% level. Accuracy is improved with the use of Agresti-Coull, Wilson, Clopper-Pearson, and a novel pseudo-count regularized bootstrap (which is particularly relevant to the calculation of F1). When texts are nested within individuals, we demonstrate that adjustment for both effective N and the appropriate degrees of freedom is necessary for producing accurate analytic intervals. Among bootstrap intervals, the hierarchical bootstrap is more accurate than the cluster bootstrap when individuals produce a moderate number of texts but overly conservative when individuals produce only a few. By providing guidance to the field on appropriate interval estimation, we aim to improve the transparency of machine learning applications, and to encourage greater attention to the validation sample size at the design stage.

</details>


### [177] [Data-driven Machine Learning Cannot Reach Symbolic-level Logical Reasoning -- The Limit of the Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26454)
*Tiansi Dong,Mateja Jamnik,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了监督深度学习因两个方法论限制（训练数据无法区分所有24种有效三段论推理，以及端到端映射引入矛盾训练目标）无法达到符号级三段论推理的严谨性，即使ChatGPT GPT-5达到100%准确率仍会给出错误解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究数据驱动的机器学习系统是否能通过增加训练数据和训练时间达到符号级三段论推理的水平，即逻辑推理的扩展律极限在哪里。

Method: 理论分析两个方法论局限，通过Euler Net实验验证，并测试ChatGPT（GPT-5-nano和GPT-5）在四种表面形式（单词、双单词、简单符号、长随机符号）下对三段论语句可满足性的判定能力。

Result: Euler Net无法实现严谨三段论推理；表面形式影响ChatGPT推理性能；GPT-5可达100%准确率但提供错误解释。

Conclusion: 监督机器学习系统无法达到符号逻辑推理的严谨性。

Abstract: Sphere neural networks have achieved symbolic level syllogistic reasoning without training data, raising the question of where the limit of the scaling law for logical reasoning lies, i.e., whether data-driven machine learning systems can achieve the same level by increasing training data and training time. We show two methodological limitations that prevent supervised deep learning from reaching the symbolic-level syllogistic reasoning: (1) training data can not distinguish all 24 types of valid syllogistic reasoning; (2) end-to-end mapping from premises to conclusion introduces contradictory training targets between neural components for pattern recognition and logical reasoning. Beside theoretical analysis, we experimentally illustrate that Euler Net cannot achieve rigorous syllogistic reasoning. We further challenge the most recent ChatGPTs (GPT-5-nano and GPT-5) to determine the satisfiability of syllogistic statements in four surface forms (patterns): words, double words, simple symbols, and long random symbols, showing that surface forms affect the reasoning performance and that ChatGPT GPT-5 may reach 100% accuracy but still provide incorrect explanations. As empirical training processes are stopped after achieving 100% accuracy, we conclude that supervised machine learning systems will not attain the rigour of symbolic logical reasoning.

</details>


### [178] [MKG-RAG-Bench: Benchmarking Retrieval in Multimodal Knowledge Graph-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26458)
*Xiaochen Wang,Bao Hoang,Han Liu,Ting Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MKG-RAG-Bench 是一个专为评估多模态知识图谱检索增强生成（MKG-RAG）中检索环节而设计的跨领域基准，涵盖通用和医学领域，实验表明多模态检索仍是关键瓶颈且直接决定生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准忽略了多模态知识图谱RAG中检索环节的挑战，而多模态知识的异构性、跨模态对齐困难和检索器设计不当使得检索成为关键瓶颈，亟需专门的评估基准。

Method: 基于通用和医学两个多模态知识图谱构建；采用基于LLM的筛选流程过滤低效用知识；生成结构化的查询及其精确监督信号；系统覆盖多种模态配置；对代表性检索器家族和多模态设置进行广泛实验。

Result: 有效的多模态检索对于端到端MKG-RAG性能仍具挑战且至关重要；检索质量强烈决定下游生成结果。

Conclusion: 将检索作为一等公民评估目标，为诊断当前局限性和推进多模态知识图谱RAG系统提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs has emerged as a promising approach for grounding large language models, yet existing benchmarks largely overlook the challenges of retrieval in multimodal knowledge graph RAG (MKG-RAG). In practice, retrieval is a critical bottleneck: multimodal knowledge is heterogeneous, difficult to align across modalities, and often poorly served by retrievers designed for unstructured corpora. To address this gap, we introduce MKG-RAG-Bench, a cross-domain benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate retrieval in MKG-RAG. MKG-RAG-Bench is constructed from two multimodal knowledge graphs spanning general and medical domains, and includes carefully aligned question-answering datasets that support controlled evaluation of both retrieval and downstream generation. The benchmark is built using an LLM-based curation pipeline that filters low-utility knowledge, generates structurally grounded queries with exact supervision, and systematically covers diverse modality configurations. Through extensive experiments across representative retriever families and modality settings, we show that effective multimodal retrieval remains challenging yet crucial for end-to-end MKG-RAG performance, and that retrieval quality strongly determines generation outcomes. By isolating retrieval as a first-class evaluation target, MKG-RAG-Bench provides a principled foundation for diagnosing current limitations and advancing multimodal knowledge graph RAG systems.

</details>


### [179] [auto-psych: Automating the science of mind using agent-driven theory discovery and experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26460)
*Ben Prystawski,Kushin Mukherjee,Daniel Wurgaft,Linas Nasvytis,Michael Y. Li,Noah D. Goodman,Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了auto-psych系统，通过嵌套的智能体发现循环自动收集众包人类数据，生成并测试计算认知科学中的理论。在内循环中推测、拟合和评判概率认知模型，外循环设计实验、在线发布并分析数据。系统能从合成数据中可靠恢复真实理论，并在三组独立人类实验中找到比文献理论拟合更好的理论，证明了自动化数据收集和理论发现在计算认知科学中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学和计算认知科学中理论生成与数据收集之间存在瓶颈，人工实验成本高、效率低。尽管AI自动化科学发现前景广阔，但将自动化技术应用于人类被试数据收集和理论发现的完整闭环尚未实现。

Method: 设计auto-psych系统，采用嵌套的双层智能体架构：内层循环负责推测、拟合和批判概率认知模型；外层循环负责设计实验（基于抛硬币序列随机性判断的经典范式）、通过众包平台在线收集人类数据并分析结果，形成自动化的闭环科学发现流程。

Result: 系统能通过系统性实验从合成数据中快速可靠地恢复真实理论，且嵌套结构对模型性能至关重要；在三组独立的人类实验中，系统发现的理论对数据的拟合优于从科学文献中提取的现有理论。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了自动化数据收集与理论发现在计算认知科学中的可行性，AI智能体系统能够自主进行假设生成、实验设计、数据收集和模型迭代，为心理学等领域的自动化科学发现提供了可行范式。

Abstract: AI-based scientific automation is increasingly possible by using agents to generate hypotheses, design experiments, and analyze data. Data collection is a major bottleneck in this pipeline, however. Psychology, and computational cognitive science in particular, is well-positioned to benefit from AI experimentation because theories are often represented as code and crowdsourcing platforms enable programmatic human data collection at scale. Here, we apply automated discovery techniques to the project of generating theories in computational cognitive science, with an agent-based system collecting human data independently through crowdsourced survey experiments. As a testbed, we use a classic case study from cognitive psychology: judging which sequences of coin flips seem subjectively more random. Our system, auto-psych, uses nested agent-based discovery loops to generate explanatory theories of human behavior. The inner loop conjectures, fits, and critiques probabilistic cognitive models; the outer loop designs experiments to test these models, launches them online, and analyzes the data.
